        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint 5 Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overview
The relevant work is concentrated in `reigh-worker`, with VibeComfy ready templates in `vibecomfy`. Current worker routing only marks `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` as VibeComfy-supported in `source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`; `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, `annotated_image_edit`, and `qwen_image` currently fail closed under VibeComfy. WGP handlers already exist for all requested Qwen/image edit task types in `source/models/model_handlers/qwen_handler.py`, but `qwen_image` appears suspicious because `TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL` maps it to `qwen_image_edit_20B` while WGP defaults include `qwen_image_20B`; do not treat it as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512`.

The cleanest plan is to make route support explicit per route, move prompt expansion and VLM lifecycle behind worker-owned wrappers, and add fixture tests before enabling any direct VibeComfy route beyond what is individually proven.

## Phase 1: Route Inventory and Fail-Closed Parity

### Step 1: Make all sprint image routes explicit (`source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add selector entries for `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
2. Keep each new route `WGP_ONLY` or `VIBECOMFY_UNSUPPORTED` until its template, inputs, LoRA handling, and runtime smoke have been proven.
3. Keep `qwen_image_2512` distinct from `qwen_image`; do not share route keys, defaults, or validation policy.
4. Extend `tests/test_template_routing.py` to assert every required route resolves explicitly and either executes only when supported or fails closed with a clear reason.

### Step 2: Validate Qwen model mapping before enabling `qwen_image` (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Audit whether `qwen_image` should use WGP model `qwen_image_20B` instead of `qwen_image_edit_20B`.
2. If WGP text-to-image generation works only with `qwen_image_20B`, update `TASK_TYPE_TO_MODEL` and the `db_task_to_generation_task()` override for `qwen_image`.
3. Add a focused unit test proving `qwen_image` and `qwen_image_2512` resolve to different models and different system prompt/Lightning LoRA paths.

### Step 3: Wire proven VibeComfy edit templates only where supported (`source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Inspect `vibecomfy/ready_templates/edit/qwen_image_edit.py` and `vibecomfy/ready_templates/image/qwen_image_2512.py` for registered input support. Current generated templates contain hard-coded prompt/image nodes, so the adapter must not route arbitrary worker tasks through them until input patching is implemented and tested.
2. Add adapter argument support only for template fields that are actually registered or patchable: prompt, seed, steps, image input, mask/composite input, and LoRA fields as applicable.
3. For `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`, prefer WGP-only until there is a concrete VibeComfy template or a safe patch layer that can express reference image, mask composite, and task LoRAs.

## Phase 2: Prompt Expansion Preprocess

### Step 4: Add a worker-owned Qwen prompt expansion service (`source/media/vlm/service.py` or new `source/media/prompt_expansion.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Implement a backend-neutral prompt expansion wrapper that calls `source.runtime.wgp_bridge.create_qwen_prompt_expander()` through the existing bridge, not from template topology.
2. Gate expansion with explicit task params such as `prompt_enhancer` or a new narrow flag if existing semantics are unclear.
3. Return structured data: original prompt, expanded prompt, provider/model metadata, and whether expansion ran.
4. Add fixture tests with a fake expander so this does not require GPU, network, or model downloads.

### Step 5: Invoke prompt expansion during task conversion (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Run prompt expansion before `QwenHandler` mutates system prompts and LoRA defaults.
2. Apply only to Qwen image/edit routes that request it; do not silently expand all prompts.
3. Preserve the original prompt in params metadata/debug fields so downstream task logs can explain what changed.
4. Add tests proving prompt expansion happens in worker preprocessing and not in VibeComfy template route selection.

## Phase 3: Backend-Neutral VLM Prompt Wrapper

### Step 6: Consolidate VLM lifecycle behind one wrapper (`source/media/vlm/service.py`, `source/media/vlm/api.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Extend the existing VLM service to expose batch helpers for transition pairs, single-image prompts, and join quads.
2. Move direct model download/expander construction out of `source/task_handlers/join/vlm_enhancement.py`, `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`, and `source/task_handlers/edit_video_orchestrator.py` into the shared service.
3. Keep fallback behavior identical: if VLM fails, callers keep existing base prompts.
4. Export `generate_single_image_prompts_batch` from `source/media/vlm/__init__.py`; travel currently imports it lazily but the namespace only re-exports transition helpers.

### Step 7: Update travel, join, and edit-video callers (`source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/join/vlm_enhancement.py`, `source/task_handlers/edit_video_orchestrator.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Replace direct Qwen/WGP VLM imports with the shared service API.
2. Keep caller-specific frame extraction and prompt mapping local to each caller.
3. Add fixture tests for travel pair prompts, single-image travel prompts, join quad prompts, and edit-video join prompts using fake service outputs.

## Phase 4: LoRA Sanitizer and Module Manifests

### Step 8: Add a LoRA sanitizer (`source/core/params/lora.py`, `source/models/lora/`, `source/utils/lora_validation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Centralize LoRA normalization for `loras`, `additional_loras`, `activated_loras`, `lora_names`, and per-phase LoRAs.
2. Sanitize URL filename decoding, Hugging Face `/blob/` to `/resolve/`, duplicate filenames, empty paths, nonnumeric scales, and architecture-incompatible LoRAs.
3. Replace the current ad hoc LTX/Wan filename filtering in `phase_config_parser.py` with the sanitizer or a shared policy function.
4. Add a fixture corpus covering Qwen, Wan, LTX, duplicate basename collisions, malformed entries, and phase multipliers.

### Step 9: Check in `module_names_<arch>.json` fixtures/manifests (`reigh-worker/` appropriate fixture/config directory)
**Scope:** Small
1. Locate the consuming code or add a small loader only if needed by the sanitizer or WGP parity checks.
2. Check in architecture-specific module name JSON files for the architectures used in this sprint, likely `qwen_image_20B`, `qwen_image_2512_20B`, and `qwen_image_edit_20B`.
3. Validate JSON shape in tests so missing or malformed manifests fail early.

## Phase 5: Verification and Parity Evidence

### Step 10: Update direct-route parity fixtures (`scripts/dual_run_compare/fixtures/`, `scripts/dual_run_compare/golden/`, tests)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Ensure golden seed payloads exist for all required routes: `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
2. Update registry snapshots/report policy so each route is either green with evidence or explicitly WGP-only.
3. Keep `qwen_image` blocked from VibeComfy routing until a distinct green observation exists.

### Step 11: Run validation in cheap-to-expensive order
**Scope:** Small
1. Start with targeted unit tests: `tests/test_template_routing.py`, `tests/test_vibecomfy_adapter.py`, VLM service tests, LoRA sanitizer tests, Qwen task conversion tests.
2. Run dual-run fixture tests under `scripts/dual_run_compare/tests/`.
3. Run broader worker tests for task conversion, WGP bridge contracts, and task registry backend selection.
4. Run live/RunPod parity only for routes being promoted to VibeComfy-supported; otherwise mark them WGP-only with fixture-backed policy.

## Execution Order
1. First make route support explicit and fail-closed so no unproven route can accidentally use VibeComfy.
2. Fix or confirm `qwen_image` WGP model mapping before adding any direct-route claims.
3. Add prompt expansion and VLM wrappers with fake-model tests before touching travel/join/edit-video callers.
4. Add LoRA sanitizer and module manifests, then update Qwen handlers and phase parsing to use the shared policy.
5. Promote routes to VibeComfy-supported only after adapter/template fixtures and runtime evidence exist.

## Validation Order
1. `uv run pytest tests/test_template_routing.py tests/test_vibecomfy_adapter.py`
2. `uv run pytest tests/test_vlm_service_contracts.py tests/test_transition_prompt_api_contracts.py` plus new VLM wrapper fixture tests.
3. `uv run pytest tests/test_lora_flow.py` plus new sanitizer fixture tests.
4. `uv run pytest tests/test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py`.
5. `uv run pytest scripts/dual_run_compare/tests`.
6. Live VibeComfy/WGP parity only for routes being moved out of WGP-only.

## Ambiguities
The main ambiguity is what “routes/patches” means for edit-mode parity: it may mean registering all route keys and marking unproven ones WGP-only, or it may require implementing VibeComfy patching for every route now. The latter depends on whether VibeComfy templates can accept worker-supplied image/mask/LoRA inputs safely.

A second ambiguity is the exact activation flag for Qwen prompt expansion. `prompt_enhancer` exists in WGP params, but the sprint says expansion belongs in worker preprocessing, so the implementation should confirm whether to reuse that flag or introduce a Qwen-specific request field.

A third ambiguity is the required schema and location for `module_names_<arch>.json`; no existing file or consumer was found in the inspected worker tree, so the implementation needs either a discovered downstream consumer or a new small manifest loader tied to LoRA sanitizer validation.

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-153",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-155",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-154",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-162",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      ]

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {

      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the broader runtime surface after the v4 path fix. sprint 2 is still moving two behavior-sensitive seams rather than just the import-path contract: the `wan2gp/` mount path and the `self_refiner` runtime. step 6.2 covers only the three `vendor_imports` getters, while the 6-file travel suite and the bridge-contract tests do not mention `self_refiner`, so dropping the dedicated self-refiner smoke from the runnable steps leaves the broader downstream surface of that changed file under-covered.",

    },

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 7,
    "plan_count": 4,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-156",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      }
    ]

    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },

        "id": "DEBT-109",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",

- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Sprint 5 Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overview
Sprint 5 should still be implemented primarily in `reigh-worker`, with `vibecomfy` touched only for ready-template input patching and validation. The critique does not point to a wrong overall target: route selection, worker preprocessing, VLM wrappers, LoRA sanitization, and parity fixtures are still the right surfaces. It does show that model selection must become the first foundation step, because route parity is meaningless if `qwen_image_2512` or `qwen_edit_model` variants resolve to unsupported or wrong WGP model strings.

Current important touch points are `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`, `source/models/model_handlers/qwen_handler.py`, `source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`, `source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py`, `source/media/vlm/`, `source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/join/vlm_enhancement.py`, and `source/task_handlers/edit_video_orchestrator.py`. The bundled WGP tree has `Wan2GP/defaults/qwen_image_2512_20B.json`, but `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py::get_supported_model_types()` does not list `qwen_image_2512_20B`; that mismatch must be resolved explicitly before claiming `qwen_image_2512` parity.

## Phase 1: Qwen Model Selection Foundation

### Step 1: Validate and fix WGP Qwen model support (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py`, `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`, `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py`, `Wan2GP/defaults/qwen_image_2512_20B.json`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Inspect the WGP default files and Qwen model handler to determine whether `qwen_image_2512_20B` is an intended model type whose support list is incomplete, or whether worker task mapping should use `qwen_image_20B` with 2512-specific weights/config another way.
2. If `qwen_image_2512_20B` is valid because its default exists, add it to WGP-supported Qwen model types/alias handling and add a test that the worker can select it without falling back.
3. If WGP cannot load `qwen_image_2512_20B` as a model type, change worker mapping to the accepted model type and preserve the 2512-specific prompt, resolution cap, and Lightning LoRA behavior in `QwenHandler.handle_qwen_image_2512()`.
4. Add tests proving `qwen_image` and `qwen_image_2512` have distinct task types, model resolution, system prompts, resolution caps, and Lightning LoRAs.

### Step 2: Apply edit variant base-model selection (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`, `source/models/model_handlers/qwen_handler.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. For `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`, set the generation model from `qwen_handler.get_edit_model_name(db_task_params)` whenever `qwen_edit_model` is present or defaults are applied.
2. Keep Lightning LoRA selection in the existing handler config, but test that the selected base model and selected Lightning LoRA variant move together for `qwen-edit`, `qwen-edit-2509`, and `qwen-edit-2511`.
3. Add regression tests around `db_task_to_generation_task()` so edit-mode callers do not silently run a plus/2511 LoRA on the base `qwen_image_edit_20B` model.

## Phase 2: Direct Route Policy and Parameter Parity

### Step 3: Make all sprint image routes explicit (`source/task_handlers/tasks/template_routing.py`, `tests/test_template_routing.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add selector entries for `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
2. Keep each unproven route `WGP_ONLY` rather than `VIBECOMFY_UNSUPPORTED` when WGP is the intended production path; fail closed under `REIGH_BACKEND=vibecomfy` with route-specific text.
3. Keep `qwen_image_2512` distinct from `qwen_image`; do not share route keys, defaults, model mapping, or validation policy.
4. Extend tests to assert every required route resolves to either VibeComfy-supported with full parameter coverage or WGP-only.

### Step 4: Add real VibeComfy input patching before keeping a route supported (`source/models/comfy/vibecomfy_adapter.py`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/image/qwen_image_2512.py`, `vibecomfy/ready_templates/edit/qwen_image_edit.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Implement a scratchpad/temporary-template patch path for VibeComfy-supported routes so worker params are not silently ignored by hard-coded generated templates.
2. Patch at least prompt, seed, steps/`num_inference_steps`, and resolution for text-to-image routes; patch image input, prompt/instruction, seed, steps, and resolution/image scale for edit routes before enabling them.
3. If resolution patching cannot be implemented for `qwen_image_2512`, demote `qwen_image_2512` from VibeComfy-supported to WGP-only/default-fixture-only until full parameter parity is available.
4. Add adapter tests that non-default resolutions no longer merely fail closed for supported routes; instead, patched scratchpads contain the requested width/height. For routes left WGP-only, tests must prove VibeComfy does not execute.

### Step 5: Promote only individually proven direct routes (`scripts/dual_run_compare/fixtures/`, `scripts/dual_run_compare/golden/`, `scripts/dual_run_compare/tests/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Ensure seed payloads and golden manifests exist for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
2. Update registry snapshots/report policies so each route is either green with evidence or explicitly WGP-only.
3. Do not promote `qwen_image` based on `qwen_image_2512`; require its own model-selection test and route evidence.

## Phase 3: Prompt Expansion Preprocess

### Step 6: Add worker-owned Qwen prompt expansion (`source/media/prompt_expansion.py`, `source/runtime/wgp_bridge.py`, `tests/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Create `source/media/prompt_expansion.py` for text prompt expansion, separate from image VLM prompt generation.
2. Use `source.runtime.wgp_bridge.create_qwen_prompt_expander()` through the bridge, never from VibeComfy template topology.
3. Gate expansion with existing `prompt_enhancer` only if current WGP semantics match the desired behavior; otherwise add a narrow explicit flag such as `qwen_prompt_expansion` and document that mapping in tests.
4. Return structured metadata: original prompt, expanded prompt, whether expansion ran, provider/model, and fallback reason.
5. Add fixture tests with a fake expander so no GPU, network, or model download is needed.

### Step 7: Invoke prompt expansion during task conversion (`source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Run prompt expansion before `QwenHandler` applies system prompts, style prompt changes, or LoRA defaults.
2. Apply only to explicitly requested Qwen image/edit routes.
3. Preserve the original prompt in generation params or debug metadata.
4. Add tests proving prompt expansion happens in worker preprocessing and no VibeComfy ready template contains prompt expansion logic.

## Phase 4: Backend-Neutral VLM Prompt Wrapper

### Step 8: Consolidate VLM lifecycle behind one worker API (`source/media/vlm/service.py`, `source/media/vlm/api.py`, `source/media/vlm/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Extend `source/media/vlm/service.py` to expose lifecycle helpers for transition pairs, single-image prompts, and join quads.
2. Export `generate_single_image_prompts_batch` from `source/media/vlm/__init__.py`; travel currently imports it lazily but the namespace only re-exports transition helpers.
3. Keep existing fallback behavior: failed VLM generation returns base prompts or `None` placeholders as today.
4. Add service tests with fake extenders and fake image inputs.

### Step 9: Update travel, join, and edit-video callers (`source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py`, `source/task_handlers/join/vlm_enhancement.py`, `source/task_handlers/edit_video_orchestrator.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Replace direct Qwen/WGP VLM model download and expander construction with the shared service API.
2. Keep caller-specific frame extraction, prompt mapping, and DB update behavior in the caller modules.
3. Add fixture tests for travel pair prompts, single-image travel prompts, join quad prompts, and edit-video join prompts.

## Phase 5: LoRA Sanitizer and Module Manifests

### Step 10: Add a LoRA sanitizer with architecture awareness (`source/models/lora/sanitizer.py`, `source/core/params/lora.py`, `source/core/params/phase_config_parser.py`, `source/utils/lora_validation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Centralize LoRA normalization for `loras`, `additional_loras`, `activated_loras`, `lora_names`, and per-phase LoRAs.
2. Sanitize URL filename decoding, Hugging Face `/blob/` to `/resolve/`, duplicate basenames, empty paths, nonnumeric scales, and phase multiplier formatting.
3. Move the current ad hoc LTX/Wan compatibility filtering in `phase_config_parser.py` into the sanitizer or a shared sanitizer policy.
4. Apply the sanitizer from both normal LoRA config parsing and phase config parsing so caller formats receive the same treatment.
5. Add a fixture corpus covering Qwen, Wan, LTX, duplicate basename collisions, malformed entries, URL normalization, and phase multipliers.

### Step 11: Check in and consume module manifests (`source/models/lora/module_manifests/module_names_<arch>.json`, `source/models/lora/module_manifest.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Create `source/models/lora/module_manifests/` as the concrete checked-in manifest location.
2. Add `module_names_qwen_image_20B.json`, `module_names_qwen_image_edit_20B.json`, `module_names_qwen_image_edit_plus_20B.json`, `module_names_qwen_image_edit_plus2_20B.json`, and the selected 2512 manifest name from Step 1.
3. Use a simple schema: `{ "architecture": "...", "module_names": ["..."], "source": "..." }`.
4. Implement `source/models/lora/module_manifest.py` to load and validate manifests by architecture, then use it from the LoRA sanitizer when architecture-specific module checks are needed.
5. Add tests that all required manifest files exist, parse, match their filename architecture, and expose non-empty `module_names` arrays.

## Phase 6: Verification and Parity Evidence

### Step 12: Run validation in cheap-to-expensive order
**Scope:** Small
1. Start with model-selection and route tests: `tests/test_template_routing.py`, new Qwen conversion/model mapping tests, and `tests/test_vibecomfy_adapter.py`.
2. Run prompt expansion and VLM wrapper fixture tests.
3. Run LoRA sanitizer and manifest tests.
4. Run existing WGP bridge and backend-selection tests.
5. Run dual-run fixture tests under `scripts/dual_run_compare/tests/`.
6. Run live/RunPod parity only for routes being promoted to VibeComfy-supported; otherwise document WGP-only policy in fixtures.

## Execution Order
1. Fix Qwen model selection first: `qwen_image_2512` support and `qwen_edit_model` variant base-model selection are blockers for all route claims.
2. Make route policy explicit and fail closed before changing VibeComfy execution.
3. Add VibeComfy scratchpad input patching for any route that remains supported; demote unsupported/default-only routes to WGP-only.
4. Add prompt expansion and VLM wrappers with fake-model tests.
5. Add LoRA sanitizer and concrete module manifests with a loader.
6. Update parity fixtures and promote routes only with route-specific evidence.

## Validation Order
1. `uv run pytest tests/test_template_routing.py tests/test_vibecomfy_adapter.py` plus new Qwen model-selection tests.
2. `uv run pytest tests/test_vlm_service_contracts.py tests/test_transition_prompt_api_contracts.py` plus new prompt expansion/VLM wrapper tests.
3. `uv run pytest tests/test_lora_flow.py` plus new sanitizer and manifest tests.
4. `uv run pytest tests/test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py tests/test_vibecomfy_backend_selection.py`.
5. `uv run pytest scripts/dual_run_compare/tests`.
6. Live VibeComfy/WGP parity only for routes moved to VibeComfy-supported.

## Ambiguities
The only remaining material ambiguity is the activation flag for Qwen prompt expansion. The implementation should first verify whether existing `prompt_enhancer` means the same behavior in worker preprocessing; if not, use a narrow Qwen-specific flag and keep `prompt_enhancer` behavior unchanged.

Whether `qwen_image_2512_20B` should be added to WGP-supported model types or mapped differently is not left ambiguous in the plan: Step 1 must resolve it by inspecting the bundled default and handler support, then adding tests for the chosen behavior.

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-156",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",

  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-154",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-162",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked vibecomfy profile wording against `vibecomfy/tests/test_ready_templates.py`. the revised plan says to add the production template to representative compile profile coverage 'alongside the existing dry-run id', but the dry-run id is not currently in `profile_smoke_template_ids`; only `video/wanvideo_wrapper_22_5b_i2v` and `video/wan_t2v` are. this is a small wording mismatch rather than a functional blocker because adding either the production id alone or both ids would still satisfy sprint 4.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-159",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "DEBT-141",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: checked the broader runtime surface after the v4 path fix. sprint 2 is still moving two behavior-sensitive seams rather than just the import-path contract: the `wan2gp/` mount path and the `self_refiner` runtime. step 6.2 covers only the three `vendor_imports` getters, while the 6-file travel suite and the bridge-contract tests do not mention `self_refiner`, so dropping the dedicated self-refiner smoke from the runnable steps leaves the broader downstream surface of that changed file under-covered.",

    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-157",
      "concern": "search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the supported direct-route scope is still broader than the adapter's guaranteed parameter surface. `db_task_to_generation_task()` currently carries direct task params such as `resolution`, `steps`, and `seed` into wgp generation, but the vibecomfy cli path only guarantees prompt, seed, steps, and memory profile; resolution remains optional/documentation-only in the plan.",

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 7,
    "plan_count": 4,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-156",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-014",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-110",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-142",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"

      {
        "id": "DEBT-154",
        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: documentation/data gap: the sprint exit criteria require profiles 1 and 3 to have vram and wall-clock data, but the plan puts that only in metadata as an info-level criterion and does not schedule a concrete artifact location or command for recording the measurements. the implementation could satisfy all unit tests while still missing one of the explicit exit criteria.",

        "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting infrastructure for resolution is still incomplete if direct vibecomfy routes are considered production-supported. vibecomfy has a `vibecomfy.patches.resolution` helper and the cli can load scratchpad files with `allow_scratchpad=true`, but the plan does not require using that path; it permits leaving resolution out for sprint 2.",

  {

    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [

        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-153",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
        ]

        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "id": "DEBT-109",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
